    GROUP PROFILE
    集團簡介



    Time Interconnect Technology Limited (the “Company”, together with             匯聚科技有限公司（「本公司」，連同其附屬公
    its subsidiaries, the “Group”) is a well-established supplier of customised    司統稱「本集團」）是一家信譽卓著的定制電線
    interconnect solutions with more than 20 years experience in the industry.     互連方案供應商 ，在行業擁有逾20年經驗 。
    The Group is headquartered in Hong Kong, and has manufacturing facilities      本集團的總部設於香港 ，並於中華人民共和國
    in Shanghai, Suzhou and Huizhou, the People’s Republic of China (“PRC“).      （「中國」）上海 、蘇州及惠州擁有生產設施 。

    The Group currently manufactures and supplies, a wide variety of              本集團目前製造及供應各種銅纜和光纜電線組
    copper & optical fiber cable assemblies and networking cables products        件及網絡電線產品 ，產品均根據個別客戶夥伴
    which are produced to the specifications and designs, of its individual       的詳述規格及設計而生產 。產品已使用在包括
    customer partners. Its products are used by a number of established           電訊 、數據中心 、工業設備 、醫療設備及網絡
    PRC and international customers in a variety of market sectors, including     電線在內的各種市場領域 ，均受不少商譽優良
    telecommunication, data centre, industrial equipment, medical equipment       的中國及國際客戶所採用 。
    and networking cables.

    Its business features on customised cable products for a variety of           我們的業務特點為定制電線產品 ，適用於各種
    specifications which are used in a wide range of applications. The Group      應用中的各種規格 。本集團按CMS基準供應電
    supplies cable products on a CMS basis, and its cable products are made-      線產品 ，有關產品根據客戶提供的規格及設計
    to-order, upon receipt of its customers’ orders.                              生產 。

    VISION                                                                        願景
    To be a leading provider of electronic interconnect products, continually      成為電纜互連產品的領先供應商 ，持續公認為
    recognised as a “Preferred Supplier” capable of delivering world class        「優選供應商」，積極地提供世界一流的品質 、
    quality, products and services – EACH and EVERY TIME.                          產品和服務 。

    STRATEGY                                                                      策略
    To become a Tier-1 supplier of cable products and high performance            成為5G電訊 、數據通訊 、醫療設備 、運輸及
    interconnect products to the market leaders in the 5G telecommunication,      工業領域的電線產品和高性能互連產品的一級
    data communication, healthcare, transport and industrial sectors.             供應商及市場領導者 。

Technology Limited (the “Company” and together with its subsidiaries,           連同其附屬公司統稱為「本集團」）截至二零二
the “Group”) for the year ended 31 March 2021 (the “Review Year” or             一年三月三十一日止年度（「回顧年度」或「二零

activities. The continued lockdown and social distancing policies have put      活動造成更多負面影響 。持續封城及社交隔離

    FINANCIAL REVIEW                                                                財務回顧
    The Group reported solid operating results and performance amid the             面對宏觀經濟環境的挑戰 ，本集團仍錄得穩健
    challenging macro-economy environment. During the Review Year, the              的經營業績及表現 。於回顧年度 ，本集團錄得
    Group recorded revenue amounting to HK$3,008.0 million, representing            的收益為3,008.0百萬港元 ，較截至二零二零
    an increase of HK$227.8 million or 8.2% as compared with HK$2,780.2             年三月三十一日止年度（「上年度」或「二零二零
    million (restated) for the year ended 31 March 2020 (the “Previous              財政年度」）的2,780.2百萬港元（經重列）增加
    Year” or “FY2020”). The increase was mainly driven by the significant           227.8百萬港元或8.2% 。有關增幅乃主要由於
    sales growth of the data centre, the medical equipment and the industrial       數據中心 、醫療設備及工業設備分部的銷售大
    equipment sector. The Group recorded a 16.1% increase in gross profit to        幅增加所致 。本集團於二零二一財政年度錄得
    HK$580.2 million in FY2021 as compared to HK$499.6 million (restated)           毛利580.2百萬港元 ，較二零二零財政年度錄
    recorded in FY2020. The gross profit margin increased from approximately        得的499.6百萬港元（經重列）增加16.1% 。毛
    18.0% (restated) for FY2020 to approximately 19.3% for FY2021. Such             利率由二零二零財政年度約18.0%（經重列）增
    increase was mainly due to the increase in revenue from the new 5G              加至二零二一財政年度約19.3% 。該增長乃主
    products of the telecommunication sector, and the increase in revenue           要是由於來自電訊分部新5G產品的收益增加 ，

    OUTLOOK                                                                         展望

PRINCIPAL RISKS AND UNCERTAINTIES                                               主要風險及不確定因素
The Directors believe that there are certain risks and uncertainties involved   董事相信 ，本集團經營業務涉及若干風險及不
in the operations of the Group, some of which are beyond the Group’s            確定因素 ，其中部分超出本集團的控制範圍 。
control. The Directors believe the more significant risks relating to the       董事相信以下為與我們業務相關的較重大風
business are as follows:                                                        險：

–   the Group may experience labour shortage or unrest or may incur high        –   本集團或會遭遇勞動力短缺或勞資糾紛或
    labour costs;                                                                   可能承擔高昂勞動成本；

–   the Group is exposed to credit risk of its customers;                       –   本集團面對客戶的信貸風險；

–   fluctuations in the prices of the Group’s major raw materials or            –   本集團的主要原材料價格或商品價格波動

    financial conditions and results of operations;                                 重不利影響；

     BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY                                                                 董事會多樣性政策

     on 28 December 2018 which sets out the basis to achieve diversity on the Board.        多樣性政策（「董事會多樣性政策」），當中載有達致董
     Details of the Board Diversity Policy is disclosed as below.                           事會多樣性的基準 。董事會多樣性政策詳情披露如

     1. Purpose                                                                             1. 目的
        The Board Diversity Policy aims to set out the approach to achieve diversity on        董事會多樣性政策旨在載列董事會成員為達致多
        the Board.                                                                             樣性而採取的方針 。

     2. Vision                                                                              2. 願景
        The Company recognises and embraces the benefits of having a diverse Board             本公司明白並深信董事會多樣性對提升本公司的
        to enhance the quality of its performance.                                             表現質素裨益良多 。

     3. Policy                                                                              3. 政策

        board diversity in terms of, among other things, gender, age, experience,              事會的多樣性 ，其中包括性別 、年齡 、經驗 、
        cultural and educational background, expertise, skills and know-how. All Board         文化及教育背景 、專業經驗 、技能及知識 。董
        appointments will be based on merits, and candidates will be considered against        事會所有委任均以用人唯才為原則 ，並在考慮人
        objective criteria, having due regard for the benefits of diversity on the Board.      選時以客觀條件充分顧及董事會多樣性的裨益 。

        diversity perspectives, including but not limited to gender, age, experience,          包括但不限於性別 、年齡 、經驗 、文化及教育
        cultural and educational background, expertise, skills and know-how.                   背景 、專業經驗 、技能及知識 。

     RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                                   風險管理及內部監控
     The Directors acknowledge that they have overall responsibility for overseeing the     董事明白彼等有全盤責任監督本公司內部監控 、財
     Company’s internal control, financial control and risk management system and shall     務監控及風險管理系統 ，並須持續監察其有效性 。
     monitor its effectiveness on an ongoing basis. A review of the effectiveness of the    董事會至少每年對風險管理及內部監控系統的有效
     risk management and internal control systems has been conducted by the Board           性進行審閱 。
     at least annually.

     Aimed at providing reasonable assurance against material errors, losses or fraud,      本公司著力提供合理保證 ，避免出現重大錯誤 、損
     the Company has established a risk management procedures which comprised the           失或造假 ，為此已成立風險管理程序 ，包括以下步
     following steps:                                                                       驟：

     –   Identify risks: Identify major and significant risks that could affect the         –   識別風險：識別可能影響達成本集團目標之主要
         achievement of goals of the Group;                                                     及重大風險；

     –   Risk assessment: Assess and evaluate the identified risk according to its likely   –   風險評估：根據已識別風險的預料影響及出現之
         impact and the likelihood of occurrence;                                               可能性作出評估及評核；

     –   Risk mitigation: Develop effective control activities to mitigate the risks.       –   紓緩風險：策劃有效的監管活動 ，務求紓緩風
                                                                                                險。

     Risk identification and assessment is performed or updated annually, and the results   每年進行或更新風險識別及評估 ，風險評估 、評核
     of risk assessment, evaluation and mitigation of each functions or operation are       的結果及各功能或營運之紓緩措施會詳細記錄在風
     documented in the Risk Registry to communicate to the Board and Management             險資料冊內 ，以供董事會及管理層審閱 。
     for reviews.

     The Group’s risk management and internal control systems are, however, designed        然而 ，本集團之風險管理及內部監控系統之設計旨
     to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives,    在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標之風險 ，僅可就
     and can only provide reasonable and not absolute assurance against material            重大失實聲明或損失提供合理而非絕對保證 。
     misstatement or loss.

     A review on the internal control systems of the Company, including financial,          獨立顧問公司內具有相關專業知識進行獨立審閱的
     operational and compliance controls and risk management functions has been             員工 ，對本公司的內部監控系統（包括財務 、營運及
     carried out by an independent consultancy company with staff in possession of          合規監控及風險管理職能）進行審閱 。
     relevant expertise to conduct an independent review.

     The Audit Committee reviewed the internal control review report issued by the          審核委員會已審閱由獨立顧問公司發佈的內部監控
     independent consultancy company and the Company’s risk management and                  審閱報告 ，以及本公司截至二零二一年三月三十一
     internal control systems in respect of the year ended 31 March 2021 and considered     日止年度的風險管理和內部監控系統 ，並認為其屬
     that they are effective and adequate. The Board assessed the effectiveness of          有效及充份 。董事會透過考慮內部監控審閱報告及
     internal control systems by considering the internal control review report and         審核委員會所進行的審閱（並同意有關審閱），評估內
     reviews performed by the Audit Committee and concurred the same.                       部監控系統的有效性 。

     The Company has no internal audit function because the Company has maintained          核職能 。本公司並無內部審核職能 ，因為本公司設

DISCLOSURE OF INSIDE INFORMATION                                                         披露內幕消息
The Group has in place a policy on disclosure of inside information which sets           本集團備有內幕消息披露政策 ，列載處理及發放內
out the procedures and internal controls for handling and dissemination of inside        幕消息的程序及內部監控 。
information.

The policy provides guidelines to the Directors, officers and all relevant employees     政策為向董事 、職員及本集團所有有關僱員提供指
of the Group to ensure proper safeguards exist to prevent the Company from               引 ，確保具恰當的保護措施 ，以免本公司違反法
breaching the statutory disclosure requirements. It also includes appropriate internal   定披露要求 。政策亦包括適當的內部監控及申報系
control and reporting systems to identify and assess potential inside information.       統 ，以辨別及評估潛在的內幕消息 。

Key procedures in place include:                                                         已設有的主要步驟包括：

–   define the requirements of periodic financial and operational reporting to the       –   向董事會及公司秘書界定定期財務及經營申報的
    Board and Company Secretary to enable them to assess inside information and              規定 ，致使彼等可評估內幕消息及（如有需要）
    make timely disclosures, if necessary;                                                   作適時披露；

–   controls the access to inside information by employees on a need-to-know             –   按須知基準控制僱員獲悉內幕消息的途徑 ，向公
    basis, and safeguarding the confidentiality of the inside information before it is       眾恰當披露時前確保內幕消息絕對保密；
    properly disclosed to public;

–   procedures of communicating with the Group’s stakeholders, including                 –   與本集團持份者（包括股東 、投資者 、分析師等）
    shareholders, investors, analysts, etc. in ways which are in compliance with the         溝通步驟 ，方式均遵從上市規則 。
    Listing Rules.

The Group has also established and implemented procedures to handle enquiries            涉及市場傳言及其他本集團事務 ，本集團已設立及
from external parties related to the market rumours and other Group’s affairs.           實行步驟 ，處理外部人士的查詢 。

To avoid uneven dissemination of inside information, the dissemination of inside         為免出現不公平發放內幕消息 ，本公司發放內幕消
information of the Company shall be conducted by publishing the relevant                 息時 ，會於聯交所網站及本公司網站刊載有關資料 。
information on the Stock Exchange’s website and the Company’s website.

Materiality Analysis                                                                            重要性分析
The Group conducts a materiality analysis to illustrate the topics of the                       本集團每年進行重要性分析 ，闡明對持份者及
greatest interest to its stakeholders and its business every year. The Group                    業務具重大利益關係的議題 。本集團使用該分
uses the outcomes of this analysis to identify material issues that enables                     析結果識別重要性議題 ，以助本集團釐定策
the Group to determine the strategies, goals and actions.                                       略 、目標及行動方案 。

The Group integrated a series of material issues based on global standards                      本集團根據全球標準及倡議 、業務情況及媒體
and initiatives, business conditions and media reports in which 18 issues                       報導綜合多項重要性議題 ，並為回顧年度訂立
were selected as the material issues of the Review Year. These issues were                      了18項的重要性議題 。根據該等議題對持份者
prioritised according to their influence on stakeholders and the business.                      及業務的影響進行重要性排序 。

After the acquisition of the networking cables business, the business and                       收購網絡電線業務後 ，本集團的業務及市場得
market of the Group have been expanded and diversified. The operation                           以擴展及多元化 。網絡電線業務及電線組件業
and production processes of the networking cables business are quite                            務兩者的營運及生產流程大相逕庭 ，前者更趨
different from the cable assembly business which are more automatic and                         自動化 ，更倚重機器 。因此18項重要性議題已
machine-oriented. As a result, the 18 material issues are re-positioned                         按目前宏觀及微觀環境重新定位 ，當中有關產
based on the current macro and micro environment in which aspects                               品 、知識產權 、供應鏈及生產管理方面的議題
related to products, intellectual property, supply chain and manufacturing                      對持份者及本集團而言愈發重要 。
management are increasingly crucial to both stakeholders and the Group.

The Environment and Natural Resources                                           環境和自然資源
To develop a green approach, minimise environmental impact and natural          為建構綠色作業 、降低對環境的影響及自然資
resources consumption, the Group has adopted a heat recovery system to          源消耗 ，本集團已採納熱回收系統 ，以回收空
recover the residual heat from the air compressor to heat up the water supply   氣壓縮機的剩餘熱量 ，為宿舍供水加熱 。此

Climate Change                                                                  氣候變化
Climate change is one of the most critical and complicated challenges           氣候變化是新世紀全球所面臨最嚴峻和複雜的
faced globally in the new century. Global warming leads to more                 挑戰之一 。全球暖化導致出現更頻繁的極端天
frequent extreme weather conditions including changes in precipitation          氣狀況 ，包括降雨模式變化 、乾旱 、水災和叢
mode, droughts, floods and bushfires. Rise in sea level will make tens of       林大火 。水位上升將引致人口稠密的沿海地區
thousands of people in densely populated coastal areas and island countries     和島國成千上萬的人流離失所 。面對各種自然
homeless. Faced with all sorts of natural disaster, individuals, corporations   災害 ，個人 、企業和政府必須立即採取行動應
and governments must take immediate actions to tackle climate change.           對氣候變化 。

Over-emission of greenhouse gases is the main factor in causing global          溫室氣體的過度排放是造成全球氣候變化的主
climate change. To achieving a low carbon economy, the Group is                 要因素 。為實現低碳經濟 ，本集團致力於通
committed to reducing its greenhouse gas emissions through the                  過緩和及適應方針減少溫室氣體排放 。舉例而
approaches of mitigation and adaptation. For example, the Group has             言 ，本集團通過採取各種環境政策和措施 ，致
strived to mitigate the risks brought by climate change through the             力減輕氣候變化所帶來的風險；並推廣節能作
adoption of various environmental policies and measures; and promoting          業 。本集團亦已考慮氣候變化對其日常營運帶
energy saving practices. The Group has also considered potential physical       來實際的潛在風險 ，如風暴 、火災或熱浪 ，並
risks of climate change to its daily operations such as storms, fires or        實施相關保護措施將風險降至最低 。
heatwaves and implemented the relevant protection measures to minimise
the risks.

that minimise any adverse impacts on the environment including reducing         量減少對環境造成的任何不利影響 ，包括減少

significant accounting policies.

1. GENERAL INFORMATION                                                                  1. 一般資料
  1.1 General information                                                                 1.1 一般資料
      Time Interconnect Technology Limited (the ”Company“) is a limited                       匯聚科技有限公司（「本公司」）為於開曼群島
      company incorporated in the Cayman Islands and its shares are listed on                 註冊成立之有限公司 ，其股份於香港聯合交
      Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                       易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市 。本公司
      Exchange”). The addresses of the registered office and the principal place              註冊辦事處及主要營業地點的地址在年報「公
      of business of the Company are disclosed in the “Corporate Information”                 司資料」一節中披露 。
      section of the annual report.

      The immediate holding company of the Company is Time Interconnect                     本公司的直接控股公司為Time Interconnect
      Holdings Limited (“Time Holdings”) which was incorporated in the British              Holdings Limited（「Time Holdings」），該公司
      Virgin Islands (“BVI”). The ultimate holding company is Linkz Industries              於英屬處女群島（「英屬處女群島」）註冊成
      Limited (“Linkz Industries”), which was incorporated in Hong Kong. Its                立 。最終控股公司為領先工業有限公司（「領
      ultimate controlling shareholder is Mr. Lo Chung Wai Paul (“Mr. Paul Lo”),            先工業」），該公司於香港註冊成立 。最終控
      who is also the non-executive director of the Company.                                股股東為羅仲煒先生（「羅仲煒先生」），其亦
                                                                                            為本公司之非執行董事 。

      The Company acts as an investment holding company. The principal                      本公司為一間投資控股公司 。其主要附屬公
      activities of its principal subsidiaries are set out in note 36.                      司之主要業務載於附註36。

        to the Conceptual Framework in HKFRS Standards” and the amendments to                  告準則中概念框架之引用的修訂本」及香港財務

        2.1 Impacts on application of Amendments to HKAS 1 and HKAS 8                          2.1 應用香港會計準則第1號及香港會計準則第8
            “Definition of Material”                                                               號修訂本「重大的定義」的影響
            The Group has applied the Amendments to HKAS 1 and HKAS 8 for the                      本集團已於本年度首次應用香港會計準則第
            first time in the current year. The amendments provide a new definition                1號及香港會計準則第8號修訂本 。該等修訂
            of material that states “information is material if omitting, misstating or            對重大提供新定義 ，當中載明「倘遺漏 、誤
            obscuring it could reasonably be expected to influence decisions that the              報或掩蓋的資料可合理預期會影響通用財務
            primary users of general purpose financial statements make on the basis                報表的主要使用者基於該等財務報表（提供
            of those financial statements, which provide financial information about               有關特定呈報實體的財務資料）所作出的決
            a specific reporting entity.” The amendments also clarify that materiality             定 ，則有關資料屬重大」。該等修訂亦釐清
            depends on the nature or magnitude of information, either individually                 重大與否視乎資料的性質或份量（單獨而言
            or in combination with other information, in the context of the financial              或與其他資料合計）對於財務報表整體而言
            statements taken as a whole.                                                           是否屬重要而定 。

            The application of the amendments in the current year had no impact on               於本年度應用該等修訂對綜合財務報表並無
            the consolidated financial statements.                                               影響 。

        2.2 Impacts on application of Amendments to HKFRS 3 “Definition of a                   2.2 應用香港財務報告準則第3號修訂本「重大的
            Business”                                                                              定義」的影響
            The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in the current year.           本集團已於本年度首次應用修訂本 。該等修
            The amendments clarify that while businesses usually have outputs, outputs             訂釐清 ，儘管業務通常具有產出 ，但就一套
            are not required for an integrated set of activities and assets to qualify as          綜合活動及資產組合而言 ，產出非屬符合業
            a business. To be considered a business, an acquired set of activities and             務定義之必要條件 。為符合業務定義 ，所
            assets must include, at a minimum, an input and a substantive process that             收購的一組活動及資產必須最少包括共同對
            together significantly contribute to the ability to create outputs.                    創造產出能力有莫大貢獻的投入及實質性流
                                                                                                   程。

            The amendments remove the assessment of whether market participants                  該等修訂移除評估市場參與者是否有能力取
            are capable of replacing any missing inputs or processes and continuing              代失去的投入或過程並繼續提供產出 。該等
            to produce outputs. The amendments also introduce additional guidance                修訂亦加入額外指引 ，有助釐定是否已獲得
            that helps to determine whether a substantive process has been acquired.             實質性過程 。

            In addition, the amendments introduce an optional concentration test that            此外 ，該等修訂加入選擇性集中度測試 ，允
            permits a simplified assessment of whether an acquired set of activities and         許對所收購的一組活動和資產是否屬於業務
            assets is not a business. Under the optional concentration test, the acquired        作簡化評估 。在該選擇性集中度測試下 ，倘
            set of activities and assets is not a business if substantially all of the fair      所收購總資產的公平值幾乎全部集中在單一
            value of the gross assets acquired is concentrated in a single identifiable          可識別資產或一組類似資產中 ，則所收購之
            asset or group of similar assets. The gross assets under assessment exclude          一組活動及資產並非業務 。評估之總資產不
            cash and cash equivalents, deferred tax assets, and goodwill resulting from          包括現金及現金等價物 、遞延稅項資產及由
            the effects of deferred tax liabilities. The election on whether to apply the        遞延稅項負債影響產生之商譽 。可按個別交
            optional concentration test is available on transaction-by-transaction basis.        易基準選擇是否應用選擇性集中度測試 。

3. BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS                             3. 綜合財務報表編製基準及主要會計政策
   AND SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
   3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated financial statements                          3.1 綜合財務報表編製基準
       Statement of compliance                                                                合規聲明
       The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in accordance                 綜合財務報表乃按照香港會計師公會頒佈之
       with HKFRSs and the principles of merger accounting under AG 5 issued                  香港財務報告準則及會計指引第5號之合併
       by the HKICPA. For the purpose of preparation of the consolidated                      會計原則編製 。就編製綜合財務報表而言 ，
       financial statements, information is considered material if such information           倘有資料合理預期將影響主要使用者所作出
       is reasonably expected to influence decisions made by primary users. In                的決定 ，則該資料可被視作重大 。此外 ，綜
       addition, the consolidated financial statements include applicable disclosures         合財務報表包括聯交所證券上市規則（「上市
       required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock                 規則」）及香港公司條例規定之適用披露 。
       Exchange (“Listing Rules”) and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

       Basis of preparation                                                                 編製基準
       The consolidated financial statements have been prepared under the                   除若干金融工具以及租賃土地及樓宇按下文
       historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments and leasehold         所闡釋會計政策以報告期末的公平值及重估
       land and buildings, which are measured at fair values and revalued amounts,          金額計量外 ，綜合財務報表按歷史成本基礎
       at the end of the reporting period, as explained in the accounting policies          編製 。
       set out below.

       Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the consideration given      歷史成本一般根據為交換貨品及服務而付出
       in exchange for goods and services.                                                  代價之公平值計算 。

       Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to           公平值是於計量日期市場參與者於有秩序交
       transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants           易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債須支付之價
       at the measurement date, regardless of whether that price is directly                格 ，而不論該價格是否可直接觀察或使用其
       observable or estimated using another valuation technique. In estimating             他估值方法估計 。於估計資產或負債之公平
       the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the          值時 ，本集團會考慮市場參與者於計量日期
       characteristics of the asset or liability if the market participants would take      對資產或負債定價時所考慮的資產或負債特
       those characteristics into account when pricing the asset or liability at the        點 。綜合財務報表中作計量及╱或披露用途
       measurement date. Fair value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes              之公平值乃按此基準釐定 ，惟於香港財務報
       in the consolidated financial statements is determined on such basis, except         告準則第2號「以股份為基礎付款」範圍內之
       for share-based payment transactions that are within the scope of HKFRS 2            以股份為基礎之付款交易 、於香港財務報告
       “Share-based Payment”, leasing transactions that are within the scope of             準則第16號範圍內之租賃交易及與公平值類
       HKFRS 16, and measurements that have some similarities to fair value but             似但並非公平值之計量（如香港會計準則第2
       are not fair value, such as net realisable value in HKAS 2 “Inventories” or          號「存貨」之可變現淨值或香港會計準則第36
       value in use in HKAS 36 “Impairment of Assets”.                                      號「資產減值」所用之使用價值）除外 。

       A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account a               非金融資產的公平值計量考慮了市場參與者
       market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using the asset        以其最高及最佳用途使用該資產或將該資產
       in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market participant that      出售予將以其最高及最佳用途使用該資產的
       would use the asset in its highest and best use.                                     另一市場參與者而產生經濟效益的能力 。

         3.2 Significant accounting policies                                                    3.2 主要會計政策
             Basis of consolidation                                                                 綜合基準
             The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial statements             綜合財務報表包括本公司及其所控制的實體
             of the Company and entities controlled by the Company (its subsidiaries).             （其附屬公司）之財務報表 。倘屬以下情況 ，
             Control is achieved when the Company:                                                  則本公司獲得控制權：

             •   has power over the investee;                                                     •   對被投資方擁有權力；

             •   is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its involvement with         •   通過對被投資方之參與面臨可變回報之
                 the investee; and                                                                    風險或取得可變回報之權利；及

             •   has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.                          •   有能力行使其權力以影響其回報 。

             The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if facts and             倘有事實及情況顯示上列三項控制權條件之
             circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more of the three            其中一項或多項有變 ，本集團會重新評估其
             elements of control listed above.                                                    是否控制被投資方 。

             Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains control over             當本集團取得附屬公司之控制權 ，便將該附
             the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses control of the subsidiary.            屬公司綜合入賬；當本集團失去附屬公司之
             Specifically, income and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of            控制權 ，便停止將該附屬公司綜合入賬 。具
             during the year are included in the consolidated statement of profit or loss         體而言 ，年內收購或出售之附屬公司之收入
             and other comprehensive income from the date the Group gains control                 及開支會自本集團取得控制權當日起計入綜
             until the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.                      合損益及其他全面收益表 ，直至本集團對該
                                                                                                  附屬公司之控制權終止當日為止 。

             Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income are attributed            損益及其他全面收入的各項目歸屬於本公司
             to the owners of the Company and to the non-controlling interests. Total             擁有人及非控股權益 。附屬公司的全面收入
             comprehensive income of subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the              總額歸屬於本公司擁有人及非控股權益 ，即
             Company and to the non-controlling interests even if this results in the non-        使此舉會導致非控股權益出現虧絀結餘 。
             controlling interests having a deficit balance.

             subsidiaries to bring their accounting policies into line with the Group’s           整 ，以使其會計政策符合本集團的會計政
             accounting policies.                                                                 策。

      Revenue from contracts with customers                                                   來自客戶合約的收益
      The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance obligation                       本集團於完成履約責任之時（或就此）確認
      is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or services underlying the                收益 ，即於特定履約責任相關貨品或服務的
      particular performance obligation is transferred to the customer.                       「控制權」移交客戶時 。

      A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a bundle of                   履約責任指特定的貨品或服務（或一組貨品
      goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct goods or services that      或服務）或一系列大致相同的特定貨品或服
      are substantially the same.                                                             務。

      Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over time by                 如符合以下其中一項標準 ，則控制權隨時間
      reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction of the relevant                 轉移 ，而收益則參照完全履行相關履約責任
      performance obligation if one of the following criteria is met:                         的進展情況而隨時間確認：

      •   the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the benefits                      •   本集團履約時 ，客戶同時取得並耗用本
          provided by the Group’s performance as the Group performs;                              集團履約所提供的利益；

      •   the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset that the                       •   本集團的履約產生或提升一項資產 ，而
          customer controls as the Group performs; or                                             該項資產於本集團履約時由客戶控制；
                                                                                                  或

      •   the Group’s performance does not create an asset with an alternative                •   本集團的履約並未產生令本集團有替代
          use to the Group and the Group has an enforceable right to payment                      用途的資產 ，且本集團對迄今已完成履
          for performance completed to date.                                                      約具有可強制執行的收取款項權利 。

      Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the customer                   否則 ，收益於客戶取得特定貨品或服務之控
      obtains control of the distinct good or service.                                        制權時確認 。

      A contract asset represents the Group’s right to consideration in exchange              合約資產指本集團就向客戶換取本集團已轉
      for goods or services that the Group has transferred to a customer that                 讓的貨品或服務收取代價的權利（尚未成為
      is not yet unconditional. It is assessed for impairment in accordance with              無條件）。其根據香港財務報告準則第9號評
      HKFRS 9. In contrast, a receivable represents the Group’s unconditional                 估減值 。相反 ，應收款項指本集團收取代價
      right to consideration, i.e. only the passage of time is required before                的無條件權利 ，即只需待時間過去代價即須
      payment of that consideration is due.                                                   到期支付 。

      A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation to transfer goods or             合約負債指本集團就已向客戶收取的代價（或
      services to a customer for which the Group has received consideration (or               代價到期的金額）而承擔向該客戶轉讓貨品
      an amount of consideration is due) from the customer.                                   或服務的責任 。

      accounted for and presented on a net basis.                                             基準入賬及呈列 。

      on a prospective basis.

      An item of property, plant and equipment is derecognised upon disposal or              物業 、廠房及設備項目會在出售或預期繼
      when no future economic benefits are expected to arise from the continued              續使用資產不會帶來未來經濟利益時解除確
      use of the asset. Any gain or loss arising on the disposal or retirement of            認 。物業 、廠房及設備項目在出售或報廢時
      an item of property, plant and equipment is determined as the difference               產生的任何收益或虧損 ，乃釐定為出售所得
      between the sales proceeds and the carrying amount of the asset and is                 款項與有關資產賬面值的差額 ，並於損益中
      recognised in profit or loss.                                                          確認 。

      Leases                                                                                 租賃
      Definition of a lease                                                                  租賃的定義
      A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right to               倘合約賦予在一段時期內控制一項已識別資
      control the use of an identified asset for a period of time in exchange for            產的使用權以換取代價 ，則該合約屬一項租
      consideration.                                                                         賃或包含一項租賃 。

      For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date of initial                 對於首次應用日期或之後訂立或修訂或業務
      application or arising from business combinations, the Group assesses                  合併產生的合約 ，本集團根據香港財務報告
      whether a contract is or contains a lease based on the definition under                準則第16號項下的定義 ，於開始日 、修訂日
      HKFRS 16 at inception, modification date or acquisition date, as appropriate.          或收購日（如適用）評估合約是否屬一項租賃
      Such contract will not be reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the            或包含一項租賃 。除非合約條款及條件在後
      contract are subsequently changed.                                                     續發生變更 ，否則不會對此類合約進行重新
                                                                                             評估 。

      The Group as a lessee                                                                  本集團作為承租人
      Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                                對合約各組成部分的代價分配
      For a contract that contains a lease component and one or more additional              對於包含租賃組成部分以及一個或多個額外
      lease or non-lease components, the Group allocates the consideration in the            租賃或非租賃組成部分的合約而言 ，本集團
      contract to each lease component on the basis of the relative stand-alone              按租賃組成部分的相對獨立價格以及非租賃
      price of the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price of the                組成部分的獨立價格合計 ，將合約的代價分
      non-lease components. Non-lease components are separated from lease                    配至各租賃組成部分 。非租賃組成部分與租
      component and are accounted for by applying other applicable standards.                賃組成部分分開 ，並採用其他適用準則入
                                                                                             賬。

      Short-term leases                                                                      短期租賃
      The Group applies the short-term lease recognition exemption to leases of              本集團對從租賃開始日租賃期為12個月或以
      staff quarters and office premises that have a lease term of 12 months or              下的租賃及不包含購買選擇權的員工宿舍租
      less from the commencement date and do not contain a purchase option.                  賃及辦公物業應用短期租賃確認豁免 。短期
      Lease payments on short-term leases are recognised as expense on a                     租賃的租賃付款在租賃期內採用直線法確認
      straight-line basis over the lease term.                                               為開支 。

      Right-of-use assets                                                                    使用權資產
      The cost of right-of-use asset includes:                                               使用權資產的成本包括：

      •   the amount of the initial measurement of the lease liability;                      •   租賃負債的初步計量金額；

      •   any lease payments made at or before the commencement date, less                   •   於開始日期或之前作出的任何租賃付
          any lease incentives received; and                                                     款 ，減任何已收租賃優惠；及

      •   any initial direct costs incurred by the Group.                                    •   本集團產生的任何初始直接成本 。

            Lease liabilities                                                                     租賃負債
            At the commencement date of a lease, the Group recognises and measures                在租賃期開始日 ，本集團按該日尚未支付的
            the lease liability at the present value of lease payments that are unpaid at         租賃付款額現值確認及計量租賃負債 。在計
            that date. In calculating the present value of lease payments, the Group              算租賃付款額的現值時 ，本集團使用租賃期
            uses the incremental borrowing rate at the lease commencement date.                   開始日的增量借款利率 。

            payments) less any lease incentives receivable.                                       付款），減去任何應收租賃優惠 。

            accretion and lease payments.                                                         賃付款作出調整 。

            changed.

                with the stand-alone price for the increase in scope and any appropriate              圍擴大對應的單獨價格 ，加上為反映特

                the particular contract.                                                              適當調整 。

            Inventories                                                                               存貨
            Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable value. Cost                存貨乃按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較低者
            is calculated using the first-in, first-out method. Net realisable value                  列賬 。成本乃採用先進先出法計算 。可變現
            represents the estimated selling price for inventories less all estimated costs           淨值指存貨的估計售價減所有估計完工成本

            Financial instruments                                                                   金融工具
            Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised when a group entity           倘集團實體成為工具合約條文的訂約方 ，則
            becomes a party to the contractual provisions of the instrument.                        金融資產及金融負債予以確認 。

            except for trade receivables arising from contracts with customers which                初步根據香港財務報告準則第15號計量之來

            that are directly attributable to the acquisition or issue of financial assets          收購或發行金融資產及金融負債而直接產生
            and financial liabilities are added to or deducted from the fair value of the           的交易成本於初始確認時於該項金融資產或
            financial assets or financial liabilities, as appropriate, on initial recognition.      金融負債的公平值中計入或扣除（如適用）。

            The effective interest method is a method of calculating the amortised cost             實際利率法乃計算金融資產或金融負債的攤
            of a financial asset or financial liability and of allocating interest income and       銷成本以及相關期間利息收入及利息開支分
            interest expense over the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the           配的方法 。實際利率乃於初始確認時按金融
            rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and payments                 資產或金融負債的預計年期或適用的較短期
            (including all fees and points paid or received that form an integral part              間準確折現估計未來現金收入及付款（包括
            of the effective interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or                 構成實際利率不可或缺部分的全部已付或已
            discounts) through the expected life of the financial asset or financial                收費用 、交易成本及其他溢價或折讓）至賬
            liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount          面淨值的利率 。
            on initial recognition.

            Classification and subsequent measurement of financial assets                           金融資產的分類及其後計量

                                                                                                    計量 。

                Interest income is recognised using the effective interest method for                   其後按攤銷成本計量的金融資產的利息

                subsequently become credit-impaired, interest income is recognised by                   言，利息收入自下一報告期間起透過
                applying the effective interest rate to the amortised cost of the financial             應用實際利率於金融資產的攤銷成本確
                asset from the next reporting period. If the credit risk on the credit-                 認 。倘已信貸減值的金融工具的信貸風
                impaired financial instrument improves so that the financial asset is no                險有所改善 ，繼而令金融資產不再信貸
                longer credit-impaired, interest income is recognised by applying the                   減值 ，利息收入則自釐定該資產並無信
                effective interest rate to the gross carrying amount of the financial asset             貸減值後的報告期間初起透過應用實際
                from the beginning of the reporting period following the determination                  利率於金融資產的總賬面值確認 。
                that the asset is no longer credit-impaired.

      (iii) Credit-impaired financial assets                                                  (iii) 信貸減值的金融資產
            A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events of default                 當發生一項或多項對金融資產估計未來

      (v) Measurement and recognition of ECL                                                (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
          The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of default, loss              預期信貸虧損的計量依據為違約概率 、
          given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and              違約損失率（即違約時的損失程度）及違
          the exposure at default. The assessment of the probability of default                 約風險的函數 。評估違約概率及違約損
          and loss given default is based on historical data and forward-looking                失率的依據是過往數據及前瞻性資料 。
          information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-                  預期信貸虧損的估計反映不偏不倚及概
          weighted amount that is determined with the respective risks of default               率加權金額 ，以各自發生違約的風險為
          occurring as the weights.                                                             權重釐定 。

          Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual cash flows             一般而言 ，預期信貸虧損為根據合約應
          that are due to the Group in accordance with the contract and the cash              付本集團的所有訂約現金流量與本集團
          flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective                預計收取的現金流量（按於初始確認時釐
          interest rate determined at initial recognition.                                    定的實際利率折現）之間的差額 。

          For a financial guarantee contract, the Group is required to make                   就財務擔保合約而言 ，本集團僅在根據
          payments only in the event of a default by the debtor in accordance                 被擔保工具的條款 ，債務人發生違約的
          with the terms of the instrument that is guaranteed. Accordingly, the               情況下方須付款 。因此 ，預期信貸虧損
          ECL is the present value of the expected payments to reimburse the                  為本集團預期就持有人發生的信貸虧損
          holder for a credit loss that it incurs less any amounts that the Group             向其作出賠付的金額 ，減去本集團預期
          expects to receive from the holder, the debtor or any other party.                  自持有人 、債務人或任何其他方收取的
                                                                                              任何金額之間的差額的現值 。

          For ECL on financial guarantee contracts for which the effective interest           對於無法釐定實際利率的財務擔保合約
          rate cannot be determined, the Group will apply a discount rate that                的預期信貸虧損 ，本集團將採用反映當
          reflects the current market assessment of the time value of money and               前市場對貨幣時間價值及現金流量特定
          the risks that are specific to the cash flows but only if, and to the extent        風險的評估的折現率 ，惟僅限於透過調
          that, the risks are taken into account by adjusting the discount rate               整折現率而非調整將予折現的現金差額
          instead of adjusting the cash shortfalls being discounted.                          來考慮風險 。

            The Group derecognises a financial asset only when the contractual rights               本集團僅於資產收取現金流量的合約權利屆
            to the cash flows from the asset expire, or when it transfers the financial             滿或轉移金融資產及其所有權的絕大部分風
            asset and substantially all the risks and rewards of ownership of the asset             險及回報予其他實體時取消確認金融資產 。
            to another entity.

            On derecognition of a financial asset at amortised cost, the difference                 於取消確認按攤銷成本計量的金融資產時 ，
            between the asset’s carrying amount and the sum of the consideration                    資產賬面值與已收及應收代價的總和間的差
            received and receivable is recognised in profit or loss.                                額 ，會於損益確認 。

            The Group derecognises financial liabilities when, and only when, the                   本集團只有在其責任獲免除 、取消或屆滿
            Group’s obligations are discharged, cancelled or have expired. The difference           時 ，方會取消確認金融負債 。獲取消確認的
            between the carrying amount of the financial liability derecognised and the             金融負債的賬面值與已付及應付代價間的差
            consideration paid and payable is recognised in profit or loss.                         額於損益確認 。

             right-of-use assets and lease liabilities are assessed on a net basis. Excess of      債有關的暫時差額按淨值基準評估 。使用權

             portion of lease liabilities results in net deductible temporary differences.         導致可扣減暫時差額淨額 。

         of the remaining benefits of the goods upon customer acceptance. For certain              間點）確認 。就若干電線組件銷售而言 ，當在履

20. CONTRACT ASSETS                                                                     20. 合約資產
   The contract assets from sales of cable assembly to a customer primarily relate        向客戶銷售電線組件的合約資產主要指本集團收
   to the Group’s right to consideration for work completed and not billed because        取已竣工而尚未收款的工程代價的權利 ，原因是
   the rights are conditioned on the Group’s future performance. The contract             該等權利乃以本集團未來表現為條件 。合約資產
   assets are transferred to trade receivables when the production of cable assembly      於電線組件產品生產完成時（即於該等權利成為
   products are completed, being when the rights become unconditional.                    無條件時）轉撥至貿易應收款項 。

         The Company’s share option scheme was adopted pursuant to a resolution                  本公司的購股權計劃（「購股權計劃」）已根據二

         available personnel, to provide additional incentive to eligible participant(s)         資格參與者 ，包括本集團僱員（全職及兼職）、

         distributors, contractors, suppliers, agents, customers, business partners or           商 、代理 、客戶 、商業夥伴或服務供應商提供
         service providers of the Group and to promote the success of the business of            額外獎勵以及推動本集團業務創出佳績 。
         the Group.

         The exercise price should be the higher of (i) the closing price of the shares of       行使價應為下列最高者：(i)授出日期（須為營業日）
         the Company as quoted in the Stock Exchange on date of grant, which must                聯交所所報本公司股份的收市價；(ii)緊接授出日
         be a business day; (ii) the average closing price of the shares of the Company as       期前五個營業日聯交所所報本公司股份的平均收
         quoted on the Stock Exchange for the 5 business days immediately preceding              市價；及(iii)授出日期本公司股份的面值 。
         the date of grant; and (iii) the nominal value of the shares of the Company on
         the date of grant.

31. RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEMES                                                          31. 退休福利計劃

   The Group operates a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme for all qualifying                 本集團為香港的所有合資格僱員參與強制性公積
   employees in Hong Kong. The assets of the scheme are held separately                    金計劃 。有關計劃的資產乃與本集團的資產分開
   from those of the Group, in funds under the control of trustees. The Group              持有 ，並存置於受託人所控制的基金 。本集團
   contributes 5% of the relevant payroll costs to the scheme, subject to a cap            向該計劃支付相關入息的5%作為供款 ，而上限
   of monthly relevant income of HK$30,000 of employees, which contribution                為僱員每月有關入息之30,000港元 ，有關供款乃
   is matched by the employees. The Group also makes voluntary contribution in             與僱員的供款一致 。除強制性供款外 ，本集團
   addition to the mandatory contribution.                                                 亦作出自願供款 。

   The employees of the Group’s subsidiaries in the PRC are members of a state-            本集團於中國附屬公司的僱員均為中國政府所營
   managed retirement benefits scheme operated by the government of the PRC.               辦的國家管理退休福利計劃的成員 。附屬公司須
   The subsidiary is required to contribute a specified percentage of payroll costs to     按工資成本的指定百分比向退休福利計劃供款以
   the retirement benefits scheme to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the         為有關福利撥資 。本集團在退休福利計劃下的唯
   Group with respect to the retirement benefits scheme is to make the specified           一責任為作出指定供款 。
   contributions.

37. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                                     37. 資本風險管理
   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will be able                為確保本集團的實體將可持續經營 ，本集團對其
   to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to stakeholders                     資本進行管理 ，並透過使債務及權益達致最佳平
   through the optimisation of the debt and equity balances. The Group’s overall                  衡為持份者締造最大回報 。本集團的整體策略於
   strategy remains unchanged throughout the year.                                                整個年度內維持不變 。

                           known companies with strong financial background.                                         的公司 ，財務實力雄厚 。

                           financial background.                                                                     厚。

       “Satisfactory” :    The counterparty has moderate default risk and                            「滿意」：           對手方違約風險輕微 ，偶爾在到
                           occasionally repays after due dates, but with fair                                        期日後償付 ，但財務實力不俗 。
                           financial background.

40. EVENT AFTER THE REPORTING PERIOD                                                           40. 報告期後事項
   On 31 May 2021, Time Investment, a directly wholly-owned subsidiary of the                         於二零二一年五月三十一日 ，本公司的直接全資
   Company, entered into a sale and purchase agreement with GP Industries                             附屬公司Time Investment與GP工業有限公司（「賣
   Limited (the “Vendor”), which owns 38.13% of Linkz Industries and is a related                     方」，擁有領先工業38.13%權益 ，為本公司的關
   party of the Company.                                                                              聯方）訂立買賣協議 。

   Pursuant to the sales and purchase agreement, Time Investment conditionally                        根據買賣協議 ，Time Investment有條件同意收
   agreed to acquire, and the Vendor conditionally agreed to sell, the entire issued                  購而賣方有條件同意出售金山工業貿易有限公
   share capital of GP Industries Marketing Limited (“GP Marketing”) at the initial                   司（「金山貿易」）全部已發行股本 ，初步代價為
   consideration of HK$69,000,000 (subject to adjustment), subject to the terms                       69,000,000港元（可予調整），受買賣協議條款及
   and conditions of the sales and purchase agreement.                                                條件規限 。

   Details of the transaction are set out in the Company’s announcement dated                         交易的詳情載於本公司日期為二零二一年五月三
